### plan-r1.md

# Plan: PGN-owned collections and derived search indexes

## Goal
Implement f-20261004-15: one authoritative library PGN for each user-owned game collection, a current derived search index, safe migration of existing copies, and accumulated account history. This is a PLAN-ONLY run at e66e2787f4987cd25fb376690f1b49736d5ced56. No implementation, ledger writes, commits, push, server or browser belong to this run.

## MANDATE
### Importing a PGN into a database makes an unlinked copy, so every user-owned collection can exist twice and the copies drift silently

* **ID:** f-20261004-15 · **Status:** open · **Area:** pgn-import · **Root:** - · **Entry:** build · **Blocked:** none
* **Where:** `src-tauri/src/db/mod.rs:986-1015` (`convert_pgn` persists only `title`/`description`; the source capability id lives only in the transient `ImportFileContext`, `mod.rs:900`), `src/components/databases/AddDatabase.tsx:56`, `src/components/databases/DatabasesPage.tsx:524-572` (Add games / Export PGN), `src/utils/tabs.ts:335-336` (`writeDbGame`), `src-tauri/src/pgn.rs:1234, 1317` (PGN writes refused off Unix), `src/components/home/AccountCard.tsx:164-190, 233-245, 315-330`, `src-tauri/src/chesscom.rs:155, 324-352, 388`, `src-tauri/src/db/mod.rs:528-531` (import skips games at or before the latest stored timestamp), `src-tauri/src/db/search.rs:518, 752-762` (mainline-only matching; unknown results omitted from stats).
* **Defect:** a PGN imported as a database is an independent, editable copy. Edits to the database copy reach search; edits to the PGN after import never reach the database, and database edits never reach the file. Felix's "Meine Partien" exists as `~/Documents/Sport/Schach/ChessFable/Eigene Partien/Meine Partien.pgn` and as a database. Account sync keeps the accumulated history only in a database and overwrites one per-account batch PGN in a download folder each sync.
* **Decision (Felix, 2026-10-04, /decide chat for `f-20261001-11`, option "One library + linked"):** every collection exists exactly once. Product rule: what is the user's lives as a PGN file in the library folder and the file is the truth; a database is either the app's search index of such a file or a standalone reference collection.
  * Game collections in the library folder get a linked search index kept current automatically, including after an external rewrite (ChessBase); edits go to the file.
  * Repertoires and studies stay plain files with no index (practice and coverage read the live tree, `RepertoireInfo.tsx:200`; the index could not see variations, `search.rs:518`). Position search inside repertoire variations is NOT promised.
  * Account sync appends only games not already present to one accumulated PGN per account in the library folder, linked like any game collection (Chess.com batches overlap by month).
  * Large collections imported from outside the library folder (Mega Database, a downloaded dump) stay standalone databases.
  * Existing unlinked file/database pairs are offered a one-time link, never silently merged; edits on either side since import are not lost.
  * Platform gate: never take away working editing. Ships where PGN write-back works (Linux, macOS); on Windows only after PGN atomic replacement is ported (reversal condition of `d-20260918-13`); until then Windows keeps unlinked databases.
* **Open question:** (technical, decided in the run) where the file↔index link is stored and how a moved/renamed/deleted source is handled; change detection (mtime/size/digest at startup or library open vs. a watcher — rule 6d); incremental vs. full re-index and its interaction with in-flight searches (`.claude/rules/async-resource-invariants.md`, `pgn-scanning.md`); write-back identity of a game inside the PGN and conflict handling against an external rewrite or a tab with unsaved edits; migration flow for existing pairs and account databases; ordering of a sync append against an external rewrite; deduplicating overlapping sync batches; whether the Windows PGN write port is part of this finding or a prerequisite.
* **Why `build`:** design across `pgn-import`, `db-search`, `native-fs` and `frontend-ui`, with a migration and a data-loss risk on write-back.
* **Depends on:** `f-20261001-11` (phase 1, the single Library page) lands first or in the same build.
* **Review record:** the recommendation was reviewed before Felix decided, orchestrator plus four Codex lenses (review-plan, review-root-cause, review-minimalism, review-pgn-index), four rounds, all APPROVED at the end. Issues found and fixed: I1 coverage was wrongly claimed to read an imported repertoire copy; I2 database search is mainline-only and omits `*` results, so repertoires get no index; I3 account-sync ownership is product-visible and was decided in the recommendation; I4 the library page alone fixes navigation, not copy drift (both problems are real); I5 drift runs in both directions; I6 review-packet completeness; I7 Windows refuses PGN writes, hence the platform gate; I8 Chess.com downloads whole months, hence deduplication; I9 wording of today's account-sync state.
* **Found by:** /decide session for `f-20261001-11`, 2026-10-04.

## Threat model and non-goals
Preserve user files, edits and account history under ordinary app use, external ChessBase rewrites, process restart, cancellation, storage refusal and concurrent in-app reads/writes. Source files may be large, malformed or contain duplicate games, custom headers, variations, NAGs and nonstandard starting positions. Keep the existing authority boundary against renderer-supplied paths and unsafe filesystem entries. Do not claim atomic exclusion against an uncooperative external writer between the existing final filesystem check and rename. d-20260924-02 explicitly retains that limitation. No new hostile local-user race-hardening program, watcher dependency, universal resource framework, variation search promise, Windows PGN port or reference-database format conversion.

## Traced premises
Frozen at BASE. Source inspection only, with reports probe-1-r1.txt and probe-2-r1.txt in RUN_TMP.

- tasks/findings.md:14096 and tasks/decisions.md:7346–7386 record the product decision, landed Library prerequisite and explicit deferral of storage changes to this finding.
- src-tauri/src/db/mod.rs:1047–1156 streams imports into one SQLite transaction, with a database write lock, DataRevision and cache invalidation. Import source identity is transient. src-tauri/src/db/create.sql:1–47 has no file provenance or source-game locator.
- src-tauri/src/db/mod.rs:3830–3940 changes database rows independently. src/utils/tabs.ts:837 dispatches database saves there. GameTable.tsx:74 and panels/database/GamesTable.tsx:43 create database origins.
- src-tauri/src/pgn.rs:1180–1235 returns exact game stamps and source revisions. Its mutation path at :1336–1445 checks source snapshots, streams unaffected bytes, atomically replaces, rebinds the installed identity and preserves committed-but-uncertain errors. Delete requires both stamp and revision. Writes remain refused off Unix at :1457 and :1540.
- src-tauri/src/file_workspace.rs:64–85 owns PGN purpose metadata, defaulting missing metadata to other. New UI-created files default to game in src/components/files/useFileWorkspace.tsx:201. Native listing reissues authority when external replacement changes identity. src-tauri/src/infra/path_authority/mod.rs:8660–8718 shows a capability ID alone does not survive that replacement.
- src-tauri/src/db/search.rs:203–374 validates SQLite search-index provenance. Search normalization at :850–880 subsequently reads SQLite, so an old mmap alone cannot guarantee a consistent result generation.
- src/state/fileFreshness.ts:278 polls open file origins every two seconds and on focus. FileFreshnessGate.tsx:135 preserves dirty trees and offers existing reload/new-game recovery. This does not monitor unopened collections.
- src/components/library/LibraryPage.tsx:25–151 composes source controllers and transient kind-qualified selection. useDatabaseLibrary.tsx exposes direct database import, export, delete, merge and cleanup actions. referenceDbAtom is consumed by local search, analysis reports and repertoire coverage.
- src/components/home/AccountCard.tsx:316–353 downloads a batch, imports into the account database and deletes empty database games. AccountCards.tsx:207,282 and home/Databases.tsx:50 find account history by database filename.
- src-tauri/src/chesscom.rs:172,345 selects inclusive months. Importer's timestamp cutoff in db/mod.rs:534 is not deduplication. Lichess native export is in fs.rs:1294. Shared batch artifact publication grants ReadPgn, not WritePgn, at fs.rs:1226.
- src/state/workspaceTypes.ts:5–10 has separate file and database origins. Existing tree stamps are stored with the tree. The workspace transaction has verified-copy-before-publish precedents in src/state/workspace.ts:417.

## Approach

### O1 — collection identity and platform boundary
Add a narrowly scoped native collection service for linked PGNs. Keep standalone database ownership and the composed Library controllers. A versioned, bounded native registry in app data records collection UUID, authorized workspace root, native-only relative source locator, current authority binding, purpose, optional provider/account identity, migration state and derived-cache identity. Do not store raw paths or credential material in renderer state. Each cache is app-owned and keyed by collection UUID, outside the standalone database directory. The registry is the link authority, not the cache or filename. Validate records at load, refuse unsupported/corrupt records without overwriting them, and surface a recoverable unavailable state. Persist using existing identified atomic-file and durability primitives. Reconcile interrupted registration by verifying the source and rebuilding disposable cache state, never inventing an empty PGN.

Automatically index files explicitly categorized as game or tournament in the authorized library workspace. Repertoire, puzzle and other files remain plain. Do not infer purpose from titles or parse content to silently reclassify existing files. Changing purpose activates or retires only the derived index, never changes the PGN bytes. App moves/renames retain UUID and update the source locator, with uncertain outcomes reconciled from native state. External replacement at the recorded locator is acquired through a fresh verified workspace listing, with a new binding and source generation. Never retarget an already-open dirty tab by pathname. An external move/rename that cannot be proven by a unique retained object identity leaves the collection unavailable until explicit source re-selection. Deleted sources remain unavailable, without silently presenting the old index as current. No inode reuse, basename or digest-only relinking across ambiguous candidates. Root changes cannot move a collection into a different workspace by spelling.

Linux and macOS enable linking. Windows preserves existing standalone import, editing and account flows, and creates no automatic linked collection or migration. Keep d-20260918-13 command-entry refusals. Centralize the support predicate in the native service and expose only typed supported/unavailable results required by real consumers. This finding does not port PGN atomic replacement.

Proof level: native persistence/restart and authority tests must show stable UUID across app rename, fresh generation after external replace, missing/ambiguous source refusal, corrupt-record preservation, and no link activation on Windows. Revert the relevant production identity or platform guard while retaining the tests and observe the expected failure.

### O2 — current and consistent derived indexes
Use full streaming rebuilds after a source generation changes. Reuse the PGN scanner and importer/encoding primitives, without importing through the account timestamp cutoff. Link each indexed row to its original source ordinal and exact game-byte stamp in that source generation. Preserve ordinal gaps for skipped malformed games and preserve identical duplicates as distinct source occurrences. The PGN remains lossless truth even when a game is not indexable. Report omissions rather than silently claiming a complete index. Do not change mainline-only position/statistics semantics.

Build rows, locators, source fingerprint and DataRevision together under the existing cancellable database write lock and a single SQLite transaction. Recheck the bound source snapshot before committing. A changed source discards the build and uses the existing three-attempt stale-read bound. A failed/canceled build rolls back and leaves the cache stale, not ready. Do not replace a live WAL database as a plain file. Search-index and result-cache provenance includes collection generation as well as database revision. A query and its later normalization must observe one generation. Before returning, verify it is still the generation admitted for that request. Stale results are discarded and refreshed or return a typed stale result, never paired with newly reordered rows.

Automatic currency checks run at app startup/library entry, window focus, after every app PGN mutation, and before any linked query or statistics consumer. Extend the existing shared two-second freshness schedule to collections actively displayed or searched, rather than adding an independent timer per component. Poll only the authority-bound identity/revision metadata, including ctime, with one outstanding check per collection. Stream a digest while rebuilding. A changed identity/revision triggers rebuilding. Inactive collections refresh at the next startup, library entry or actual use. No index that failed the admitted source check is served as current. Bound jobs through the existing native operation capacity, deduplicate rebuilds by collection/generation, release transient entries on all terminal paths, retain only the current cache generation and clean owned abandoned artifacts on startup. Disk-full cleanup errors remain surfaced and retryable without deleting the source.

Proof level: real native PGN→SQLite→position/game/statistics consumers must distinguish old and new games after external same-size/mtime-preserving rewrite, app save, append and deletion. Race rebuild/search/normalization and source rewrite, plus cancellation, restart and index-write failure. Assert no mixed generation, no partial publication, correct duplicate ordinals and bounded streaming on a 300 MB fixture. Source-only faults removing source revalidation, transaction rollback or generation validation must make corresponding assertions fail.

### O3 — one edit authority through every entry point
Linked search results resolve to the live file-backed opening path, using collection/generation/ordinal/stamp together. Read original PGN bytes for the tab, rather than reconstructing an editable game from lossy database columns. Carry the source revision and stamp with the admitted tree. If a result is obsolete, refuse/refresh it before tab admission. Preserve both ordinary file and linked-result freshness, dirty-tree recovery, app-write suppression, close-save and uncertain-write retry protections. Persist any added identity fields through schema validation and an explicit backward-compatible migration. Existing database tabs are re-resolved against native collection state on hydration and before save, so an already-open pre-link tab cannot overwrite the newly derived index. Preserve its dirty tree for the existing recovery action instead.

All linked content mutations go through the PGN mutation facade: save, add/import games, selected-game delete, cleanup and player edits that change game text. Keep source generation plus ordinal/stamp checks for destructive edits, including identical duplicates and shifted ordinals. Route bulk linked transformations through one streaming PGN transaction preserving unrelated bytes and metadata, then refresh the cache. At native database entry points, refuse attempts to write linked cache rows directly even if a stale renderer still holds a handle. Do not depend on hidden buttons for enforcement. Derived-index rebuild/drop controls may change only derived artifacts. Reference databases retain their existing mutation behavior. Linked export uses the source PGN, while explicit standalone-reference export keeps its existing semantics. Deleting a derived index cannot delete the source collection. Collection deletion uses the existing Library trash/restore contract.

Proof level: production GameTable and board-panel opening paths, save/close-save, linked delete/add-games/cleanup/player editing, and stale database-handle native calls. Assert file bytes change first, search follows, custom headers/variations/NAGs/FEN survive, dirty tabs remain intact on external rewrite, and an obsolete equal-text result cannot edit/delete another occurrence. Source-only bypass faults must turn these preservation assertions red.

### O4 — one Library item and preserved search consumers
Compose linked file and its derived database as one Library collection. The file row owns selection and file actions, with Explore/search and index status on the same item. Suppress its cache from standalone database listings. Keep existing source-specific controllers, shared search/type filtering, keyboard/focus interaction and route compatibility. Keep /library and /databases/$databaseId according to d-20261013-01. Explorer resolution must obtain the current authorized index for that collection, not trust persisted cache handles. Rebuild/error/missing-source states withhold actionable search results and explain retry or source re-selection using existing feedback patterns. Keep plain repertoire/study behavior and live-tree practice/coverage.

Reconcile referenceDbAtom, active database view, SWR caches, home statistics and all native preload/search/report consumers on generation change, relink or index retirement. A linked reference resolves through the same currency check. Preserve standalone reference selection, signed catalog downloads and external large-collection imports. No cache can reappear as a second user-owned collection after reload or a failed refresh. Route all renderer native access through src/platform and registered Specta commands/events. Use existing ProgressEvent/operation correlation. Regenerate bindings and prove them byte-exact. Translate every new visible string through i18next.

Proof level: composed renderer tests and canonical container screenshots covering linked/plain/reference items, stale/error/missing states, keyboard selection, filtering, old explorer URLs and reference consumers. Actual Tauri acceptance must prove a Library edit changes PGN bytes and an ensuing real search sees the change. Unlinking UI suppression or native collection resolution in source must fail the corresponding assertion.

### O5 — explicit one-time migration without lost versions
Offer linking for existing file/database pairs without inferring an association from a title. Let the user explicitly select both authoritative capabilities. Remember completed/declined offers by stable identities, with an explicit link action still available. Before activation, show counts/status and let the user select the PGN or database version as the future source. Do not attempt a silent game-level merge. For a PGN choice, preserve the complete prior database in a recoverable archive. For a database choice, first preserve the complete prior PGN and database, export the database into a new library PGN with exclusive creation, and use that new file as source. Leave the original versions recoverable and clearly separate from active collections. Never overwrite either original as part of a link offer.

Use a durable migration intent with source identities/revisions, verified recovery-artifact ownership, intended destination and activation state. Copy/flush/verify recovery data before activation, then atomically publish the registry link and retire the old active database only after that publication. Account associations, selected references, explorer state, recent entries and open tabs are reconciled after publication. Dirty tabs remain recoverable and cannot save to a retired independent copy. A failure before activation retains the old usable state. An uncertain activation is reported as such and reconciled at restart without repeating destructive steps. Decline/cancel changes no data. Re-running or crashing at any boundary cannot overwrite backups, duplicate activation or lose an original. Native archive copies use a consistent SQLite snapshot including WAL state, not a raw file copy of an open database. Archives are excluded from normal listings and have an explicit restore path. Do not silently merge or delete user edits.

Proof level: native restart/fault-injection tests around every durable migration boundary, with distinct edits on both original sides, WAL-active databases, dirty pre-link tabs, canceled offers, failed backup/registry writes and repeated execution. Assert original-version recovery and exactly one active linked item. Composed migration UI and actual Tauri acceptance must reach the same production native flow. Faulting backup-before-activation or idempotent activation must fail preservation assertions.

### O6 — accumulated and deduplicated account PGNs
On supported platforms, resolve account history through the native collection registry using provider plus canonical account identity. Download into owned private staging, append only absent games into one accumulated library PGN, and refresh its linked index through O2. Retain native-only credentials, origin restrictions, request tickets, per-account ownership and cancellation. Never retain the batch as a second user-owned collection or grant a downloaded ReadPgn artifact write authority. Reuse one batch-append domain operation for Library add-games and both providers, with account-specific identity policy passed as data.

Use provider plus stable game ID from validated provider URL/tags when present. When no provider ID exists, use a deterministic semantic fingerprint of starting position, mainline, players and game date/time, preserving existing source bytes and annotations for matches. Scope fingerprints to the account/provider. Do not collapse distinct provider IDs with identical moves or remove pre-existing duplicate source occurrences. Maintain dedupe information in rebuildable on-disk index state, not an unbounded renderer set. Overlapping monthly batches and repeated completed batches append once. Timestamp is an inclusive fetch optimization, never the import admission rule. Refetch the latest covered interval inclusively so tied timestamps and monthly overlap are deduped by identity, not discarded by <= time. A full initial import is required when history has no proven coverage cursor.

Network staging happens outside the source edit lock. After download, acquire the shared PGN edit owner, re-read the current source generation and derive absent games from that generation. One streamed atomic append publishes the new PGN. External rewrite between preparation and commit produces the existing stale/conflict outcome and preserves both source and downloaded staging for safe retry. Cancellation before publication changes no file. After publication, completion distinguishes committed PGN with pending/failed index refresh from no append. The file is authoritative on restart and retry re-derives dedupe membership, so lost receipts or uncertain index completion cannot append the batch twice. Cancellation/account removal/app shutdown clean or retain owned staging with explicit recovery and bounded retention. Never automatically retry an uncertain non-idempotent write.

Existing account databases receive O5's explicit migration. Preserve all accumulated history before switching, and reconcile AccountCards/home personal summaries to the registry identity. Windows continues the current database sync, with no removal of working editing. Logout does not delete a user's accumulated PGN. Provider/schema/network failures are typed, renderer-safe, terminal and retryable only before safe/idempotent boundaries. Test provider response fixtures through the actual transport seams without real credentials or live account writes.

Proof level: native real-byte append with overlapping Chess.com months, equal timestamps, distinct IDs with identical moves, modified source annotations, missing provider IDs, interrupted append/index completion and external rewrite. Renderer account action/remount/cancel/error tests must observe durable counts and terminal ownership. Real Tauri acceptance uses deterministic native transport fixtures and verifies accumulated PGN bytes plus resulting search. Source-only removal of membership checks, current-source revalidation or commit-outcome distinction must fail the matching assertions.

## Decisions and trade-offs
Decided autonomously under full auto, proposals for adopter recording after its adoption gate:

- Optimal: native UUID registry plus disposable app-owned SQLite caches → I recommend this instead of persisting a source pathname in an independently editable database. UUID is stable when capabilities are renewed. Reversal: migrate registry records while keeping PGNs and rebuilding caches.
- Optimal: full transactional streaming rebuild with source-qualified queries → I recommend this over incremental edit synchronization and live WAL-file swaps. Correctness covers arbitrary ChessBase rewrites. Reversal: replace rebuild internals after the same generation/isolation proofs.
- Reuse the existing two-second metadata freshness schedule and focus/use checks, not a watcher dependency. Reversal: replace change notification while retaining source revalidation and the stated refresh bound.
- Keep Windows unlinked under d-20260918-13. PGN atomic replacement is a prerequisite to a separate Windows rollout, not part of this implementation.
- Migration selects one version explicitly and archives both originals before activation, rather than attempting an unpromised merge. Reversal: restore archived originals and remove the registry link through the native service.
- Account identity deduplication uses provider IDs first and a documented semantic fallback when absent, with source content preserved on a match. Reversal: rebuild the derived membership projection without changing previously stored PGN bytes.
- Preserve d-20261013-01/02/03/04's Library composition and transient selection. Linked storage is new authorized scope explicitly reserved by d-20261013-04. No universal reference/file backend is introduced.

## Risks / open questions
No unsettled product question is required by the recorded mandate. External rename without provable identity needs explicit re-selection. Existing per-game editing has the acknowledged external check-to-rename race. Full rebuild latency and temporary disk space are visible operation states, not permission to show stale results as current. Native macOS behavior and Windows preservation require their real CI jobs, Linux cross-compilation is insufficient. All runtime/large-file/crash proof remains unrun in PLAN-ONLY.

## Not part of this task
No co-located joiner planning, including f-20261010-27. No unrelated scanner repairs, credential redesign, engine work, gate machinery, new custom source verifier, automatic pair inference, silent merge, reference database conversion or Windows write port. Existing plain files are not auto-classified from names.

## Phases

### Phase 1 — authoritative collection lifecycle, all production entry points
One cohesive source-of-truth phase, implemented by a selected-executor sensitive write leaf. Native link/index identity, file-backed writes, migration and account appends share the same registry, generation and IPC contract. They must be integrated before this phase is committed, rather than shipping a cache that old write entry points can mutate independently. The worker may structure the implementation by native collection core, renderer integration and account/migration consumers, sequentially within this phase. Root owns design, arbitration, diff inspection, independent proof, commits and release. No concurrent writers or shared-index operations.

Existing owned source scopes: src-tauri/src/{main,error,pgn,file_workspace,chesscom,fs}.rs, src-tauri/src/db/, src-tauri/src/infra/{path_authority,fs,operations}*, src/platform/, src/bindings/generated.ts through generation, src/components/{library,files,databases,home,common/AccountCards,panels/database,panels/analysis/ReportModal,panels/practice/RepertoireInfo,tabs}/, src/state/{atoms,fileFreshness,pathOwners,workspace,workspaceTypes}*, src/state/store/{database,tree,tabStorage}*, src/utils/{db,files,tabs,pathCapabilities}*, src/utils/{chess.com,lichess}/, src/translation/, e2e/{database-files,file-freshness,accounts-puzzles-engines,workspace-tabs}.spec.ts and affected snapshots, scripts/verify-app.mjs, CHESS_LOGIC_MAP.md and docs/localization.md if needed. New collection-domain modules/tests belong beside the corresponding native, renderer or test owners. Do not broaden native filesystem/HTTP/CSP scope or change reference DB schemas merely to host linked-only metadata. Gate/coverage mapping may register new production modules without lowering floors or budgets.

Phase proof commands, run in order with exit status checked, using existing wrappers:

1. pnpm bindings:generate
2. pnpm i18n:extract
3. pnpm gate:ensure backend-test
4. pnpm gate:ensure frontend-coverage
5. pnpm bindings:check
6. pnpm checks:pre-review
7. pnpm gates:contract:check

New native tests live under a linked_collections test filter and exercise O1–O6 through production cores and real disposable files/databases. Run cargo test --manifest-path src-tauri/Cargo.toml --locked --bin chessfable linked_collections for each source-fault red/restore/green proof, plus affected existing pgn, file_workspace, db, account and authority tests through backend-test. The frontend-coverage gate must include composed O3–O6 tests and exact affected importers, not only mocks of the collection facade. Record each semantic source reversal and exact assertion/status, then restore and rerun the complete integrated proof. Verification-artifact assertions added to verify-app follow push-review-policy §2's individual staged-failure evidence requirement. No bespoke source scanner.

After phase proof/commit, run cumulative diff review with correctness, root-cause, tests, error-handling, minimalism and triggered project lenses, plus a records lens for records. All adopted Fixes require proof and closure before visual acceptance or gates. Run pnpm test:e2e:container first, inspect diffs, update only intended snapshots in the canonical container, and name them. Run pnpm build then pnpm verify:app against disposable data, extending the existing harness for O4–O6 and staging each new assertion. Native GTK dialogs remain outside automated acceptance, without delegating routine page/native behavior to Felix.

Final adopter release: record autonomous decisions and full review history via their authorized routes, commit known records, then run pnpm gates:push -- --rust --frontend --bindings on the clean reviewed tree. Preserve coverage/bundle ratchets. Check newest remote jobs, push only through the project skill and expected target, wait for pushed-SHA rust-windows-test, rust-macos-test and rust-platform jobs, then install according to the push skill. Never claim this planner ran those gates or released changes. Close f-20261004-15 only after all O1–O6 are implemented and verified. Co-located candidates are revalidated after implementation and receive their own fixes outside this plan.

## Reviews
Pending round 1. Plan authorship and arbitration share one root context. Detection runs on the same OpenAI model family as the code, with fresh executor sessions only.

### plan-r2.md

# Plan: PGN-owned collections and derived search indexes

## Goal
Implement f-20261004-15: one authoritative library PGN for each user-owned game collection, a current derived search index, safe migration of existing copies, and accumulated account history. This is a PLAN-ONLY run at e66e2787f4987cd25fb376690f1b49736d5ced56. No implementation, ledger writes, commits, push, server or browser belong to this run.

## MANDATE
### Importing a PGN into a database makes an unlinked copy, so every user-owned collection can exist twice and the copies drift silently

* **ID:** f-20261004-15 · **Status:** open · **Area:** pgn-import · **Root:** - · **Entry:** build · **Blocked:** none
* **Where:** `src-tauri/src/db/mod.rs:986-1015` (`convert_pgn` persists only `title`/`description`; the source capability id lives only in the transient `ImportFileContext`, `mod.rs:900`), `src/components/databases/AddDatabase.tsx:56`, `src/components/databases/DatabasesPage.tsx:524-572` (Add games / Export PGN), `src/utils/tabs.ts:335-336` (`writeDbGame`), `src-tauri/src/pgn.rs:1234, 1317` (PGN writes refused off Unix), `src/components/home/AccountCard.tsx:164-190, 233-245, 315-330`, `src-tauri/src/chesscom.rs:155, 324-352, 388`, `src-tauri/src/db/mod.rs:528-531` (import skips games at or before the latest stored timestamp), `src-tauri/src/db/search.rs:518, 752-762` (mainline-only matching; unknown results omitted from stats).
* **Defect:** a PGN imported as a database is an independent, editable copy. Edits to the database copy reach search; edits to the PGN after import never reach the database, and database edits never reach the file. Felix's "Meine Partien" exists as `~/Documents/Sport/Schach/ChessFable/Eigene Partien/Meine Partien.pgn` and as a database. Account sync keeps the accumulated history only in a database and overwrites one per-account batch PGN in a download folder each sync.
* **Decision (Felix, 2026-10-04, /decide chat for `f-20261001-11`, option "One library + linked"):** every collection exists exactly once. Product rule: what is the user's lives as a PGN file in the library folder and the file is the truth; a database is either the app's search index of such a file or a standalone reference collection.
  * Game collections in the library folder get a linked search index kept current automatically, including after an external rewrite (ChessBase); edits go to the file.
  * Repertoires and studies stay plain files with no index (practice and coverage read the live tree, `RepertoireInfo.tsx:200`; the index could not see variations, `search.rs:518`). Position search inside repertoire variations is NOT promised.
  * Account sync appends only games not already present to one accumulated PGN per account in the library folder, linked like any game collection (Chess.com batches overlap by month).
  * Large collections imported from outside the library folder (Mega Database, a downloaded dump) stay standalone databases.
  * Existing unlinked file/database pairs are offered a one-time link, never silently merged; edits on either side since import are not lost.
  * Platform gate: never take away working editing. Ships where PGN write-back works (Linux, macOS); on Windows only after PGN atomic replacement is ported (reversal condition of `d-20260918-13`); until then Windows keeps unlinked databases.
* **Open question:** (technical, decided in the run) where the file↔index link is stored and how a moved/renamed/deleted source is handled; change detection (mtime/size/digest at startup or library open vs. a watcher — rule 6d); incremental vs. full re-index and its interaction with in-flight searches (`.claude/rules/async-resource-invariants.md`, `pgn-scanning.md`); write-back identity of a game inside the PGN and conflict handling against an external rewrite or a tab with unsaved edits; migration flow for existing pairs and account databases; ordering of a sync append against an external rewrite; deduplicating overlapping sync batches; whether the Windows PGN write port is part of this finding or a prerequisite.
* **Why `build`:** design across `pgn-import`, `db-search`, `native-fs` and `frontend-ui`, with a migration and a data-loss risk on write-back.
* **Depends on:** `f-20261001-11` (phase 1, the single Library page) lands first or in the same build.
* **Review record:** the recommendation was reviewed before Felix decided, orchestrator plus four Codex lenses (review-plan, review-root-cause, review-minimalism, review-pgn-index), four rounds, all APPROVED at the end. Issues found and fixed: I1 coverage was wrongly claimed to read an imported repertoire copy; I2 database search is mainline-only and omits `*` results, so repertoires get no index; I3 account-sync ownership is product-visible and was decided in the recommendation; I4 the library page alone fixes navigation, not copy drift (both problems are real); I5 drift runs in both directions; I6 review-packet completeness; I7 Windows refuses PGN writes, hence the platform gate; I8 Chess.com downloads whole months, hence deduplication; I9 wording of today's account-sync state.
* **Found by:** /decide session for `f-20261001-11`, 2026-10-04.

## Threat model and non-goals
Preserve user files, edits and account history under ordinary app use, external ChessBase rewrites, process restart, cancellation, storage refusal and concurrent in-app reads/writes. Source files may be large, malformed or contain duplicate games, custom headers, variations, NAGs and nonstandard starting positions. Keep the existing authority boundary against renderer-supplied paths and unsafe filesystem entries. Do not claim atomic exclusion against an uncooperative external writer between the existing final filesystem check and rename. d-20260924-02 explicitly retains that limitation. No new hostile local-user race-hardening program, watcher dependency, universal resource framework, variation search promise, Windows PGN port or reference-database format conversion.

Frozen at BASE. Source inspection only, with reports probe-1-r1.txt and probe-2-r1.txt in RUN_TMP.

## Approach

### O1 — collection identity and platform boundary
Add a narrowly scoped native collection service for linked PGNs. Keep standalone database ownership and the composed Library controllers. A versioned, bounded native registry in app data records collection UUID, authorized workspace root, native-only relative source locator, current authority binding, purpose, optional provider/account identity, migration state and derived-cache identity. Do not store raw paths or credential material in renderer state. Each cache is app-owned and keyed by collection UUID, outside the standalone database directory. The registry is the link authority, not the cache or filename. Validate records at load, refuse unsupported/corrupt records without overwriting them, and surface a recoverable unavailable state. Persist using existing identified atomic-file and durability primitives. Reconcile interrupted registration by verifying the source and rebuilding disposable cache state, never inventing an empty PGN.

Automatically index files explicitly categorized as game or tournament in the authorized library workspace. Repertoire, puzzle and other files remain plain. Do not infer purpose from titles or parse content to silently reclassify existing files. Changing purpose activates or retires only the derived index, never changes the PGN bytes. App moves/renames retain UUID and update the source locator, with uncertain outcomes reconciled from native state. External replacement at the recorded locator is acquired through a fresh verified workspace listing, with a new binding and source generation. Never retarget an already-open dirty tab by pathname. An external move/rename that cannot be proven by a unique retained object identity leaves the collection unavailable until explicit source re-selection. Deleted sources remain unavailable, without silently presenting the old index as current. No inode reuse, basename or digest-only relinking across ambiguous candidates. Root changes cannot move a collection into a different workspace by spelling.

Linux and macOS enable linking. Windows preserves existing standalone import, editing and account flows, and creates no automatic linked collection or migration. Keep d-20260918-13 command-entry refusals. Centralize the support predicate in the native service and expose only typed supported/unavailable results required by real consumers. This finding does not port PGN atomic replacement.

Proof level: native persistence/restart and authority tests must show stable UUID across app rename, fresh generation after external replace, missing/ambiguous source refusal, corrupt-record preservation, and no link activation on Windows. Revert the relevant production identity or platform guard while retaining the tests and observe the expected failure.

### O2 — current and consistent derived indexes
Use full streaming rebuilds after a source generation changes. Reuse the PGN scanner and importer/encoding primitives, without importing through the account timestamp cutoff. Link each indexed row to its original source ordinal and exact game-byte stamp in that source generation. Preserve ordinal gaps for skipped malformed games and preserve identical duplicates as distinct source occurrences. The PGN remains lossless truth even when a game is not indexable. Report omissions rather than silently claiming a complete index. Do not change mainline-only position/statistics semantics.

Build rows, locators, source fingerprint and DataRevision together under the existing cancellable database write lock and a single SQLite transaction. Recheck the bound source snapshot before committing. A changed source discards the build and uses the existing three-attempt stale-read bound. A failed/canceled build rolls back and leaves the cache stale, not ready. Do not replace a live WAL database as a plain file. Search-index and result-cache provenance includes collection generation as well as database revision. A query and its later normalization must observe one generation. Before returning, verify it is still the generation admitted for that request. Stale results are discarded and refreshed or return a typed stale result, never paired with newly reordered rows.

Automatic currency checks run at app startup/library entry, window focus, after every app PGN mutation, and before any linked query or statistics consumer. Extend the existing shared two-second freshness schedule to collections actively displayed or searched, rather than adding an independent timer per component. Poll only the authority-bound identity/revision metadata, including ctime, with one outstanding check per collection. Stream a digest while rebuilding. A changed identity/revision triggers rebuilding. Inactive collections refresh at the next startup, library entry or actual use. No index that failed the admitted source check is served as current. Bound jobs through the existing native operation capacity, deduplicate rebuilds by collection/generation, release transient entries on all terminal paths, retain only the current cache generation and clean owned abandoned artifacts on startup. Disk-full cleanup errors remain surfaced and retryable without deleting the source.

Proof level: real native PGN→SQLite→position/game/statistics consumers must distinguish old and new games after external same-size/mtime-preserving rewrite, app save, append and deletion. Race rebuild/search/normalization and source rewrite, plus cancellation, restart and index-write failure. Assert no mixed generation, no partial publication, correct duplicate ordinals and bounded streaming on a 300 MB fixture. Source-only faults removing source revalidation, transaction rollback or generation validation must make corresponding assertions fail.

### O3 — one edit authority through every entry point
Linked search results resolve to the live file-backed opening path, using collection/generation/ordinal/stamp together. Read original PGN bytes for the tab, rather than reconstructing an editable game from lossy database columns. Carry the source revision and stamp with the admitted tree. If a result is obsolete, refuse/refresh it before tab admission. Preserve both ordinary file and linked-result freshness, dirty-tree recovery, app-write suppression, close-save and uncertain-write retry protections. Persist any added identity fields through schema validation and an explicit backward-compatible migration. Existing database tabs are re-resolved against native collection state on hydration and before save, so an already-open pre-link tab cannot overwrite the newly derived index. Preserve its dirty tree for the existing recovery action instead.

All linked content mutations go through the PGN mutation facade: save, add/import games, selected-game delete, cleanup and player edits that change game text. Keep source generation plus ordinal/stamp checks for destructive edits, including identical duplicates and shifted ordinals. Route bulk linked transformations through one streaming PGN transaction preserving unrelated bytes and metadata, then refresh the cache. At native database entry points, refuse attempts to write linked cache rows directly even if a stale renderer still holds a handle. Do not depend on hidden buttons for enforcement. Derived-index rebuild/drop controls may change only derived artifacts. Reference databases retain their existing mutation behavior. Linked export uses the source PGN, while explicit standalone-reference export keeps its existing semantics. Deleting a derived index cannot delete the source collection. Collection deletion uses the existing Library trash/restore contract.

Proof level: production GameTable and board-panel opening paths, save/close-save, linked delete/add-games/cleanup/player editing, and stale database-handle native calls. Assert file bytes change first, search follows, custom headers/variations/NAGs/FEN survive, dirty tabs remain intact on external rewrite, and an obsolete equal-text result cannot edit/delete another occurrence. Source-only bypass faults must turn these preservation assertions red.

### O4 — one Library item and preserved search consumers
Compose linked file and its derived database as one Library collection. The file row owns selection and file actions, with Explore/search and index status on the same item. Suppress its cache from standalone database listings. Keep existing source-specific controllers, shared search/type filtering, keyboard/focus interaction and route compatibility. Keep /library and /databases/$databaseId according to d-20261013-01. Explorer resolution must obtain the current authorized index for that collection, not trust persisted cache handles. Rebuild/error/missing-source states withhold actionable search results and explain retry or source re-selection using existing feedback patterns. Keep plain repertoire/study behavior and live-tree practice/coverage.

Reconcile referenceDbAtom, active database view, SWR caches, home statistics and all native preload/search/report consumers on generation change, relink or index retirement. A linked reference resolves through the same currency check. Preserve standalone reference selection, signed catalog downloads and external large-collection imports. No cache can reappear as a second user-owned collection after reload or a failed refresh. Route all renderer native access through src/platform and registered Specta commands/events. Use existing ProgressEvent/operation correlation. Regenerate bindings and prove them byte-exact. Translate every new visible string through i18next.

Proof level: composed renderer tests and canonical container screenshots covering linked/plain/reference items, stale/error/missing states, keyboard selection, filtering, old explorer URLs and reference consumers. Actual Tauri acceptance must prove a Library edit changes PGN bytes and an ensuing real search sees the change. Unlinking UI suppression or native collection resolution in source must fail the corresponding assertion.

### O5 — explicit one-time migration without lost versions
Offer linking for existing file/database pairs without inferring an association from a title. For a pair, let the user explicitly select both authoritative capabilities. Existing account history also supports database-only migration when no old batch PGN exists: select the account database, archive it, export its complete history into an exclusively created library PGN, and link that new file. Never require a disposable prior download batch to recover accumulated history. Remember completed/declined offers by stable identities, with an explicit link action still available. Before activation, show counts/status and let the user select the PGN or database version as the future source where both exist. Do not attempt a silent game-level merge. For a PGN choice, preserve the complete prior database in a recoverable archive. For a database choice, first preserve the complete prior database and any selected prior PGN, export the database into a new library PGN with exclusive creation, and use that new file as source. Leave the original versions recoverable and clearly separate from active collections. Never overwrite either original as part of a link offer.

Use a durable migration intent with source identities/revisions, verified recovery-artifact ownership, intended destination and activation state. Copy/flush/verify recovery data before activation, then atomically publish the registry link and retire the old active database only after that publication. Account associations, selected references, explorer state, recent entries and open tabs are reconciled after publication. Dirty tabs remain recoverable and cannot save to a retired independent copy. A failure before activation retains the old usable state. An uncertain activation is reported as such and reconciled at restart without repeating destructive steps. Decline/cancel changes no data. Re-running or crashing at any boundary cannot overwrite backups, duplicate activation or lose an original. Native archive copies use a consistent SQLite snapshot including WAL state, not a raw file copy of an open database. Archives are excluded from normal listings and have an explicit restore path. Do not silently merge or delete user edits.

Proof level: native restart/fault-injection tests around every durable migration boundary, with distinct edits on both original sides, WAL-active databases, dirty pre-link tabs, canceled offers, failed backup/registry writes and repeated execution. Include an account database with accumulated history and no batch PGN, migrated and restarted before overlapping sync. Assert original-version recovery, full accumulated history and exactly one active linked item. Composed migration UI and actual Tauri acceptance must reach the same production native flow. Faulting backup-before-activation, database-only migration or idempotent activation must fail preservation assertions.

### O6 — accumulated and deduplicated account PGNs
On supported platforms, resolve account history through the native collection registry using provider plus canonical account identity. Download into owned private staging, append only absent games into one accumulated library PGN, and refresh its linked index through O2. Retain native-only credentials, origin restrictions, request tickets, per-account ownership and cancellation. Never retain the batch as a second user-owned collection or grant a downloaded ReadPgn artifact write authority. Reuse one batch-append domain operation for Library add-games and both providers, with account-specific identity policy passed as data. Remove the Lichess games-times-900 estimate from the renderer/native IPC and exact-size contract on both supported linked and Windows legacy paths. Derive progress from an actual response length where present, otherwise retain indeterminate progress. Preserve all actual Content-Length, operation byte-limit, signature/digest and explicit exact-size checks for catalog/engine downloads. This resolves the production initial-sync prerequisite observed in existing f-20261004-19 without a new size-policy abstraction or weakened artifact validation.

Use provider plus stable game ID from validated provider URL/tags when present, and also derive a deterministic semantic fingerprint of starting position, mainline, players and game date/time for matching across old/new representations. Existing database import/export drops identity tags such as Link. Therefore an incoming ID-bearing game must also be compared to compatible ID-less migrated occurrences through the semantic fingerprint. A uniquely matched legacy occurrence counts as already present while its bytes and annotations remain unchanged. Preserve occurrence multiplicity and never match two distinct known provider IDs solely because their fingerprints agree. Missing fields, conflicting identity evidence or multiple indistinguishable legacy candidates must produce an explicit reconciliation conflict before append rather than guessing, dropping games or claiming success. Reconciliation must preserve the original source, downloaded batch and both versions, using the existing conflict/recovery pattern. No provider-ID assignment may be reused after source-generation changes without revalidation against the current source. Scope fingerprints to the account/provider. Do not remove pre-existing duplicate source occurrences. Maintain dedupe information in rebuildable on-disk index state, not an unbounded renderer set. Overlapping monthly batches and repeated completed batches append once. Timestamp is an inclusive fetch optimization, never the import admission rule. Refetch the latest covered interval inclusively so tied timestamps and monthly overlap are deduped by identity, not discarded by <= time. A full initial import is required when history has no proven coverage cursor.

Network staging happens outside the source edit lock. After download, acquire the shared PGN edit owner, re-read the current source generation and derive absent games from that generation. One streamed atomic append publishes the new PGN. External rewrite between preparation and commit produces the existing stale/conflict outcome and preserves both source and downloaded staging for safe retry. Cancellation before publication changes no file. After publication, completion distinguishes committed PGN with pending/failed index refresh from no append. The file is authoritative on restart and retry re-derives dedupe membership, so lost receipts or uncertain index completion cannot append the batch twice. Cancellation/account removal/app shutdown clean or retain owned staging with explicit recovery and bounded retention. Never automatically retry an uncertain non-idempotent write.

Existing account databases receive O5's explicit migration. Preserve all accumulated history before switching, and reconcile AccountCards/home personal summaries to the registry identity. Windows continues the current database sync, with no removal of working editing. Logout does not delete a user's accumulated PGN. Provider/schema/network failures are typed, renderer-safe, terminal and retryable only before safe/idempotent boundaries. Test provider response fixtures through the actual transport seams without real credentials or live account writes.

Proof level: native real-byte append with overlapping Chess.com months, equal timestamps, distinct IDs with identical moves, modified source annotations, missing provider IDs, interrupted append/index completion and external rewrite. Feed a provider-ID-bearing fixture through the real database importer/exporter, migrate it, restart, then sync the overlapping provider body and assert zero already-present games are appended. Include ambiguous legacy matches that preserve all bytes and terminally report reconciliation, plus distinct known IDs that both survive. Drive the real Lichess download runtime with a valid body whose length differs from games-times-900 and assert initial sync succeeds with and without Content-Length. Verify signed/catalog exact-size mismatch still refuses. Renderer account action/remount/cancel/error tests must observe durable counts and terminal ownership. Real Tauri acceptance uses deterministic native transport fixtures and verifies accumulated PGN bytes plus resulting search. Source-only removal of mixed-identity matching, membership checks, current-source revalidation or commit-outcome distinction, and reinstating the estimate as an exact-size constraint, must fail the matching assertions.

## Decisions and trade-offs
Decided autonomously under full auto, proposals for adopter recording after its adoption gate:

- Optimal: native UUID registry plus disposable app-owned SQLite caches → I recommend this instead of persisting a source pathname in an independently editable database. UUID is stable when capabilities are renewed. Reversal: migrate registry records while keeping PGNs and rebuilding caches.
- Optimal: full transactional streaming rebuild with source-qualified queries → I recommend this over incremental edit synchronization and live WAL-file swaps. Correctness covers arbitrary ChessBase rewrites. Reversal: replace rebuild internals after the same generation/isolation proofs.
- Reuse the existing two-second metadata freshness schedule and focus/use checks, not a watcher dependency. Reversal: replace change notification while retaining source revalidation and the stated refresh bound.
- Keep Windows unlinked under d-20260918-13. PGN atomic replacement is a prerequisite to a separate Windows rollout, not part of this implementation.
- Migration selects one version explicitly and archives both originals before activation, rather than attempting an unpromised merge. Reversal: restore archived originals and remove the registry link through the native service.
- Account identity deduplication compares known IDs and compatible legacy fingerprints, preserving source content, multiplicity and distinct known IDs. Ambiguous identities require reconciliation before append. Reversal: rebuild the derived membership projection without changing previously stored PGN bytes.
- Remove the inaccurate Lichess byte estimate and use real response length or indeterminate progress. Rejected treating an estimate as an exact integrity promise, and a new size-policy abstraction for one removed caller. Reversal: introduce a distinct nonbinding progress hint only if an evidenced consumer needs it, with exact-size rejection tests preserved.
- Preserve d-20261013-01/02/03/04's Library composition and transient selection. Linked storage is new authorized scope explicitly reserved by d-20261013-04. No universal reference/file backend is introduced.

## Risks / open questions
No unsettled product question is required by the recorded mandate. External rename without provable identity needs explicit re-selection. Existing per-game editing has the acknowledged external check-to-rename race. Full rebuild latency and temporary disk space are visible operation states, not permission to show stale results as current. Native macOS behavior and Windows preservation require their real CI jobs, Linux cross-compilation is insufficient. All runtime/large-file/crash proof remains unrun in PLAN-ONLY.

## Not part of this task
No co-located joiner planning, including f-20261010-27. No unrelated scanner repairs, credential redesign, engine work, gate machinery, new custom source verifier, automatic pair inference, silent merge, reference database conversion or Windows write port. Existing plain files are not auto-classified from names.

## Phases

### Phase 1 — authoritative collection lifecycle, all production entry points
One cohesive source-of-truth phase, implemented by a selected-executor sensitive write leaf. Native link/index identity, file-backed writes, migration and account appends share the same registry, generation and IPC contract. They must be integrated before this phase is committed, rather than shipping a cache that old write entry points can mutate independently. The worker may structure the implementation by native collection core, renderer integration and account/migration consumers, sequentially within this phase. Root owns design, arbitration, diff inspection, independent proof, commits and release. No concurrent writers or shared-index operations.

Existing owned source scopes: src-tauri/src/{main,error,pgn,file_workspace,chesscom,fs}.rs, src-tauri/src/db/, src-tauri/src/infra/{path_authority,fs,operations}*, src/platform/, src/bindings/generated.ts through generation, src/components/{library,files,databases,home,common/AccountCards,panels/database,panels/analysis/ReportModal,panels/practice/RepertoireInfo,tabs}/, src/state/{atoms,fileFreshness,pathOwners,workspace,workspaceTypes}*, src/state/store/{database,tree,tabStorage}*, src/utils/{db,files,tabs,pathCapabilities}*, src/utils/{chess.com,lichess}/, src/translation/, e2e/{database-files,file-freshness,accounts-puzzles-engines,workspace-tabs}.spec.ts and affected snapshots, scripts/verify-app.mjs, CHESS_LOGIC_MAP.md and docs/localization.md if needed. New collection-domain modules/tests belong beside the corresponding native, renderer or test owners. Do not broaden native filesystem/HTTP/CSP scope or change reference DB schemas merely to host linked-only metadata. Gate/coverage mapping may register new production modules without lowering floors or budgets.

Phase proof commands, run in order with exit status checked, using existing wrappers:

1. pnpm bindings:generate
2. pnpm i18n:extract
3. pnpm gate:ensure backend-test
4. pnpm gate:ensure frontend-coverage
5. pnpm bindings:check
6. pnpm checks:pre-review
7. pnpm gates:contract:check

New native tests live under a linked_collections test filter and exercise O1–O6 through production cores and real disposable files/databases. Run cargo test --manifest-path src-tauri/Cargo.toml --locked --bin chessfable linked_collections for each source-fault red/restore/green proof, plus affected existing pgn, file_workspace, db, account and authority tests through backend-test. The frontend-coverage gate must include composed O3–O6 tests and exact affected importers, not only mocks of the collection facade. Record each semantic source reversal and exact assertion/status, then restore and rerun the complete integrated proof. Verification-artifact assertions added to verify-app follow push-review-policy §2's individual staged-failure evidence requirement. No bespoke source scanner.

After phase proof/commit, run cumulative diff review with correctness, root-cause, tests, error-handling, minimalism and triggered project lenses, plus a records lens for records. All adopted Fixes require proof and closure before visual acceptance or gates. Run pnpm test:e2e:container first, inspect diffs, update only intended snapshots in the canonical container, and name them. Run pnpm build then pnpm verify:app against disposable data, extending the existing harness for O4–O6 and staging each new assertion. Native GTK dialogs remain outside automated acceptance, without delegating routine page/native behavior to Felix.

Final adopter release: record autonomous decisions and full review history via their authorized routes, commit known records, then run pnpm gates:push -- --rust --frontend --bindings on the clean reviewed tree. Preserve coverage/bundle ratchets. Check newest remote jobs, push only through the project skill and expected target, wait for pushed-SHA rust-windows-test, rust-macos-test and rust-platform jobs, then install according to the push skill. Never claim this planner ran those gates or released changes. Close f-20261004-15 only after all O1–O6 are implemented and verified. Co-located candidates are revalidated after implementation and receive their own fixes outside this plan.

### plan-r3.md

# Plan: PGN-owned collections and derived search indexes

## Goal
Implement f-20261004-15: one authoritative library PGN for each user-owned game collection, a current derived search index, safe migration of existing copies, and accumulated account history. This is a PLAN-ONLY run at e66e2787f4987cd25fb376690f1b49736d5ced56. No implementation, ledger writes, commits, push, server or browser belong to this run.

## MANDATE
### Importing a PGN into a database makes an unlinked copy, so every user-owned collection can exist twice and the copies drift silently

* **ID:** f-20261004-15 · **Status:** open · **Area:** pgn-import · **Root:** - · **Entry:** build · **Blocked:** none
* **Where:** `src-tauri/src/db/mod.rs:986-1015` (`convert_pgn` persists only `title`/`description`; the source capability id lives only in the transient `ImportFileContext`, `mod.rs:900`), `src/components/databases/AddDatabase.tsx:56`, `src/components/databases/DatabasesPage.tsx:524-572` (Add games / Export PGN), `src/utils/tabs.ts:335-336` (`writeDbGame`), `src-tauri/src/pgn.rs:1234, 1317` (PGN writes refused off Unix), `src/components/home/AccountCard.tsx:164-190, 233-245, 315-330`, `src-tauri/src/chesscom.rs:155, 324-352, 388`, `src-tauri/src/db/mod.rs:528-531` (import skips games at or before the latest stored timestamp), `src-tauri/src/db/search.rs:518, 752-762` (mainline-only matching; unknown results omitted from stats).
* **Defect:** a PGN imported as a database is an independent, editable copy. Edits to the database copy reach search; edits to the PGN after import never reach the database, and database edits never reach the file. Felix's "Meine Partien" exists as `~/Documents/Sport/Schach/ChessFable/Eigene Partien/Meine Partien.pgn` and as a database. Account sync keeps the accumulated history only in a database and overwrites one per-account batch PGN in a download folder each sync.
* **Decision (Felix, 2026-10-04, /decide chat for `f-20261001-11`, option "One library + linked"):** every collection exists exactly once. Product rule: what is the user's lives as a PGN file in the library folder and the file is the truth; a database is either the app's search index of such a file or a standalone reference collection.
  * Game collections in the library folder get a linked search index kept current automatically, including after an external rewrite (ChessBase); edits go to the file.
  * Repertoires and studies stay plain files with no index (practice and coverage read the live tree, `RepertoireInfo.tsx:200`; the index could not see variations, `search.rs:518`). Position search inside repertoire variations is NOT promised.
  * Account sync appends only games not already present to one accumulated PGN per account in the library folder, linked like any game collection (Chess.com batches overlap by month).
  * Large collections imported from outside the library folder (Mega Database, a downloaded dump) stay standalone databases.
  * Existing unlinked file/database pairs are offered a one-time link, never silently merged; edits on either side since import are not lost.
  * Platform gate: never take away working editing. Ships where PGN write-back works (Linux, macOS); on Windows only after PGN atomic replacement is ported (reversal condition of `d-20260918-13`); until then Windows keeps unlinked databases.
* **Open question:** (technical, decided in the run) where the file↔index link is stored and how a moved/renamed/deleted source is handled; change detection (mtime/size/digest at startup or library open vs. a watcher — rule 6d); incremental vs. full re-index and its interaction with in-flight searches (`.claude/rules/async-resource-invariants.md`, `pgn-scanning.md`); write-back identity of a game inside the PGN and conflict handling against an external rewrite or a tab with unsaved edits; migration flow for existing pairs and account databases; ordering of a sync append against an external rewrite; deduplicating overlapping sync batches; whether the Windows PGN write port is part of this finding or a prerequisite.
* **Why `build`:** design across `pgn-import`, `db-search`, `native-fs` and `frontend-ui`, with a migration and a data-loss risk on write-back.
* **Depends on:** `f-20261001-11` (phase 1, the single Library page) lands first or in the same build.
* **Review record:** the recommendation was reviewed before Felix decided, orchestrator plus four Codex lenses (review-plan, review-root-cause, review-minimalism, review-pgn-index), four rounds, all APPROVED at the end. Issues found and fixed: I1 coverage was wrongly claimed to read an imported repertoire copy; I2 database search is mainline-only and omits `*` results, so repertoires get no index; I3 account-sync ownership is product-visible and was decided in the recommendation; I4 the library page alone fixes navigation, not copy drift (both problems are real); I5 drift runs in both directions; I6 review-packet completeness; I7 Windows refuses PGN writes, hence the platform gate; I8 Chess.com downloads whole months, hence deduplication; I9 wording of today's account-sync state.
* **Found by:** /decide session for `f-20261001-11`, 2026-10-04.

## Threat model and non-goals
Preserve user files, edits and account history under ordinary app use, external ChessBase rewrites, process restart, cancellation, storage refusal and concurrent in-app reads/writes. Source files may be large, malformed or contain duplicate games, custom headers, variations, NAGs and nonstandard starting positions. Keep the existing authority boundary against renderer-supplied paths and unsafe filesystem entries. Do not claim atomic exclusion against an uncooperative external writer between the existing final filesystem check and rename. d-20260924-02 explicitly retains that limitation. No new hostile local-user race-hardening program, watcher dependency, universal resource framework, variation search promise, Windows PGN port or reference-database format conversion.

## Traced premises
Frozen at BASE. Source inspection only, with reports probe-1-r1.txt and probe-2-r1.txt in RUN_TMP.

- tasks/findings.md:14096 and tasks/decisions.md:7346–7386 record the product decision, landed Library prerequisite and explicit deferral of storage changes to this finding.
- src-tauri/src/db/mod.rs:1047–1156 streams imports into one SQLite transaction, with a database write lock, DataRevision and cache invalidation. Import source identity is transient. src-tauri/src/db/create.sql:1–47 has no file provenance or source-game locator.
- src-tauri/src/db/mod.rs:3830–3940 changes database rows independently. src/utils/tabs.ts:837 dispatches database saves there. GameTable.tsx:74 and panels/database/GamesTable.tsx:43 create database origins.
- src-tauri/src/pgn.rs:1180–1235 returns exact game stamps and source revisions. Its mutation path at :1336–1445 checks source snapshots, streams unaffected bytes, atomically replaces, rebinds the installed identity and preserves committed-but-uncertain errors. Delete requires both stamp and revision. Writes remain refused off Unix at :1457 and :1540.

Native listing reissues authority when external replacement changes identity. src-tauri/src/infra/path_authority/mod.rs:8660–8718 shows a capability ID alone does not survive that replacement.
- src-tauri/src/db/search.rs:203–374 validates SQLite search-index provenance. Search normalization at :850–880 subsequently reads SQLite, so an old mmap alone cannot guarantee a consistent result generation.

- src/components/library/LibraryPage.tsx:25–151 composes source controllers and transient kind-qualified selection. useDatabaseLibrary.tsx exposes direct database import, export, delete, merge and cleanup actions. referenceDbAtom is consumed by local search, analysis reports and repertoire coverage.
- src/components/home/AccountCard.tsx:316–353 downloads a batch, imports into the account database and deletes empty database games. AccountCards.tsx:207,282 and home/Databases.tsx:50 find account history by database filename.
